            const mainCount = (tournament.main ? tournament.main.length : 0) + (tournament.wc ? tournament.wc.length : 0);
            const qualCount = (tournament.qual ? tournament.qual.length : 0) + (tournament.qualWc ? tournament.qualWc.length : 0);
            const altCount = tournament.alt ? tournament.alt.length : 0;
            const qualAltCount = tournament.qualAlt ? tournament.qualAlt.length : 0;
            const hasMainPdf = !!tournament.mainPdf;
            const isQualOnly = mainCount === 0 && !hasMainPdf && !tournament.mainDelayed && (qualCount > 0 || qualAltCount > 0);
            var html = '<div class="tournament-card"><div class="tournament-header" onclick="toggleTournament(this)"><div><div class="tournament-name">' + displayName + '</div><div class="tournament-counts">' + surfaceBadge;
            if (tournament.mainDelayed) html += '<span class="count-badge" style="background:#5a1c1c;color:#ff6b6b;">Delayed</span>';
            else if (hasMainPdf && mainCount > 0) html += '<span class="count-badge" style="background:#2d5a1c;color:#7ddf64;">MD Draw</span><span class="count-badge">MD: ' + mainCount + '</span>';
            else if (hasMainPdf) html += '<span class="count-badge" style="background:#2d5a1c;color:#7ddf64;">MD Draw</span>';
            else if (!isQualOnly) html += '<span class="count-badge">MD: ' + mainCount + '</span>';
            if (tournament.qualPdf) html += '<span class="count-badge" style="background:#2d5a1c;color:#7ddf64;">Q Draw</span>';
            else if (qualCount > 0) html += '<span class="count-badge">Q: ' + qualCount + '</span>';
            if (altCount > 0) html += '<span class="count-badge">NEXT: ' + altCount + '</span>';
            if (qualAltCount > 0) html += '<span class="count-badge">Q-NEXT: ' + qualAltCount + '</span>';
            html += '</div></div><span class="toggle-icon">▼</span></div><div class="tournament-content">';
            if (tournament.mainDelayed) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><span style="display:inline-block;padding:0.6rem 1rem;background:#5a1c1c;color:#ff6b6b;border-radius:6px;font-size:0.85rem;font-weight:600;">⚠ Main Draw Delayed</span></div>';
            } else if (mainCount === 0 && !hasMainPdf && qualCount === 0 && altCount === 0 && qualAltCount === 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="text-align:center;padding:1.5rem 1rem;color:#888;font-style:italic;">Entry Lists Not Yet Released</div>';
            } else if (mainCount > 0) {
                if (hasMainPdf) html += '<div class="entry-section" style="text-align:center;padding:0.75rem 1rem 0;"><a href="' + tournament.mainPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Main Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Main Draw</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                const mdEntries = (tournament.main || []).map(p => [p, false]).concat((tournament.wc || []).map(p => [p, true]));
                const mdKey = e => (typeof e[0][0] === 'number' && !['CO','JR','NG'].some(t => e[0].includes(t))) ? e[0][0] : Infinity;
                mdEntries.sort((a, b) => mdKey(a) - mdKey(b));
                mdEntries.forEach(e => { html += playerRow(e[0], e[1]); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            } else if (hasMainPdf) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><a href="' + tournament.mainPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Main Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
            }
            if (tournament.qualPdf) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><a href="' + tournament.qualPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Qualifying Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
            } else if (qualCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                if (tournament.qual) tournament.qual.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                if (tournament.qualWc) tournament.qualWc.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, true); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (altCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">' + ((tournament.name && tournament.name.includes('Qualifying')) ? 'Qualifying Draw Next-In' : 'Main Draw Next-In') + '</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.alt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (qualAltCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying Next-In</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.qualAlt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

                if (tournament.qual) tournament.qual.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                if (tournament.qualWc) tournament.qualWc.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, true); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (altCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">' + ((tournament.name && tournament.name.includes('Qualifying')) ?                 tournament.alt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (qualAltCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying Next-In</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.qualAlt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="text-align:center;padding:1.5rem 1rem;color:#888;font-style:italic;">Entry Lists Not Yet Released</div>';
            } else if (mainCount > 0) {
                if (hasMainPdf) html += '<div class="entry-section" style="text-align:center;padding:0.75rem 1rem 0;"><a href="' + tournament.mainPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Main Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Main Draw</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                const mdEntries = (tournament.main || []).map(p => [p, false]).concat((tournament.wc || []).map(p => [p, true]));
                const mdKey = e => (typeof e[0][0] === 'number' && !['CO','JR','NG'].some(t => e[0].includes(t))) ? e[0][0] : Infinity;
                mdEntries.sort((a, b) => mdKey(a) - mdKey(b));
                mdEntries.forEach(e => { html += playerRow(e[0], e[1]); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            } else if (hasMainPdf) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><a href="' + tournament.mainPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Main Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
            }
            if (tournament.qualPdf) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><a href="' + tournament.qualPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Qualifying Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
            } else if (qualCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                if (tournament.qual) tournament.qual.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                if (tournament.qualWc) tournament.qualWc.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, true); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (altCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">' + ((tournament.name && tournament.name.includes('Qualifying')) ? 'Qualifying Draw Next-In' : 'Main Draw Next-In') + '</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.alt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (qualAltCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying Next-In</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.qualAlt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

            html += '</div></div><span class="toggle-icon">▼</span></div><div class="tournament-content">';
            if (tournament.mainDelayed) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><span style="display:inline-block;padding:0.6rem 1rem;background:#5a1c1c;color:#ff6b6b;border-radius:6px;font-size:0.85rem;font-weight:600;">⚠ Main Draw Delayed</span></div>';
            } else if (mainCount === 0 && !hasMainPdf && qualCount === 0 && altCount === 0 && qualAltCount === 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="text-align:center;padding:1.5rem 1rem;color:#888;font-style:italic;">Entry Lists Not Yet Released</div>';
            } else if (mainCount > 0) {
                if (hasMainPdf) html += '<div class="entry-section" style="text-align:center;padding:0.75rem 1rem 0;"><a href="' + tournament.mainPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Main Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Main Draw</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                const mdEntries = (tournament.main || []).map(p => [p, false]).concat((tournament.wc || []).map(p => [p, true]));
                const mdKey = e => (typeof e[0][0] === 'number' && !['CO','JR','NG'].some(t => e[0].includes(t))) ? e[0][0] : Infinity;
                mdEntries.sort((a, b) => mdKey(a) - mdKey(b));
                mdEntries.forEach(e => { html += playerRow(e[0], e[1]); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            } else if (hasMainPdf) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><a href="' + tournament.mainPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Main Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
            }
            if (tournament.qualPdf) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section" style="padding:0.75rem;"><a href="' + tournament.qualPdf + '" target="_blank" class="pdf-btn">Qualifying Draw PDF ↗</a></div>';
            } else if (qualCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                if (tournament.qual) tournament.qual.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                if (tournament.qualWc) tournament.qualWc.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, true); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (altCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">' + ((tournament.name && tournament.name.includes('Qualifying')) ? 'Qualifying Draw Next-In' : 'Main Draw Next-In') + '</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.alt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (qualAltCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying Next-In</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.qualAlt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }

 ="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                if (tournament.qual) tournament.qual.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                if (tournament.qualWc) tournament.qualWc.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, true); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (altCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">' + ((tournament.name && tournament.name.includes('Qualifying')) ?                 tournament.alt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            if (qualAltCount > 0) {
                html += '<div class="entry-section"><div class="entry-section-title">Qualifying Next-In</div><table class="entry-table"><thead><tr><th class="rank-col">#</th><th>Player</th><th class="country-col">Ctry</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
                tournament.qualAlt.forEach(p => { html += playerRow(p, false); });
                html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            }
            html += '</div></div>';
            return html;
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }

        function renderWeek(weekData, weekNum) {
            const tiers = ['gs', 'atp1000', 'atp500', 'atp250', 'atp125', 'itf'];
            tiers.forEach(tier => {
                const section = document.getElementById(tier + '-section-week' + weekNum);
                const grid = document.getElementById(tier + '-week' + weekNum);
                if (!section || !grid) return;
                const tournaments = weekData[tier] || [];
                if (tournaments.length > 0) {
                    section.style.display = '';
                    grid.innerHTML = tournaments.map(t => createTournamentCard(t)).join('');
                }
            });
        }
